...STORE_CANDIDATES.filter(candidate=>!VERIFIED_STORES.some(store=>store.role!=="EXCLU"&&Math.abs(store.km-candidate.km)<1.5)) ].sort((a,b)=>a.km-b.km); let autonomy = 2; let storeSearchMode = 'autonomy'; let lastShown = null; function havKm(a,b){ const p1=rad(a[0]), p2=rad(b[0]), dp=p2-p1, dl=rad(b[1]-a[1]); const h=Math.sin(dp/2)**2+Math.cos(p1)*Math.cos(p2)*Math.sin(dl/2)**2; return 2*(R/1000)*Math.asin(Math.sqrt(h)); } function buildSegments(){ const segs=[]; let routeKm=0; DATA.tracks.forEach((pts,i)=>{ const stage=i+1; for(let j=0;j=storeKm)return days; } return 90; } function select(km,stage){ const usable=ALL_STORES.filter(s=>s.role!=="EXCLU" && s.role!=="A_QUALIFIER"); if(storeSearchMode==='nearest'){ const nearest=usable.filter(s=>s.km>km).sort((a,b)=>a.km-b.km)[0]; if(!nearest){ return {verdict:"FIN",role:"FIN",store:null,windowEnd:DATA.routeLengthKm,endStage:90,nearestMode:true}; } const required=daysNeededForStore(km,stage,nearest.km); return { verdict:nearest.role==="CANDIDATE"?"√Ä V√âRIFIER":"PLUS PROCHE", role:nearest.role,store:nearest,requiredDays:required,windowEnd:nearest.km, endStage:stageFromKm(nearest.km),nearestMode:true,candidateTiming:nearest.role==="CANDIDATE"?"nearest":null }; } const windowEnd=autonomyWindowEnd(km,stage,autonomy); const if(normal) return {verdict:"OK",role:"NORMAL",store:normal,windowEnd,endStage}; const secours=usable.filter(s=>s.role==="SECOURS"&&onTargetDay(s)).sort((a,b)=>b.km-a.km)[0]; if(secours) return {verdict:"OK",role:"SECOURS",store:secours,windowEnd,endStage}; const candidate=usable.filter(s=>s.role==="CANDIDATE"&&onTargetDay(s)).sort((a,b)=>b.km-a.km)[0]; if(candidate) return {verdict:"√Ä V√âRIFIER",role:"CANDIDATE",store:candidate,requiredDays:autonomy,windowEnd,endStage,candidateTiming:"target"}; const earlier=usable .filter(s=>s.role==="NORMAL"&&s.km>km&&daysNeededForStore(km,stage,s.km)b.km-a.km)[0]; if(earlier){ const required=daysNeededForStore(km,stage,earlier.km); return {verdict:"PLUS T√îT",role:"NORMAL",store:earlier,requiredDays:required,windowEnd,endStage}; } const earlierSecours=usable .filter(s=>s.role==="SECOURS"&&s.km>km&&daysNeededForStore(km,stage,s.km)b.km-a.km)[0]; if(earlierSecours){ const required=daysNeededForStore(km,stage,earlierSecours.km); return {verdict:"PLUS T√îT",role:"SECOURS",store:earlierSecours,requiredDays:required,windowEnd,endStage}; } const earlierCandidate=usable .filter(s=>s.role==="CANDIDATE"&&s.km>km&&daysNeededForStore(km,stage,s.km)b.km-a.km)[0]; if(earlierCandidate){ const required=daysNeededForStore(km,stage,earlierCandidate.km); return {verdict:"√Ä V√âRIFIER",role:"CANDIDATE",store:earlierCandidate,requiredDays:required,windowEnd,endStage,candidateTiming:"earlier"}; } const next=usable .filter(s=>s.km>km&&daysNeededForStore(km,stage,s.km)>autonomy) .sort((a,b)=>a.km-b.km)[0]; if(!next) return {verdict:"FIN",role:"FIN",store:null,windowEnd,endStage}; const required=daysNeededForStore(km,stage,next.km); return {verdict:required>4?">4 JOURS":"HORS FEN√äTRE",role:next.role,store:next,requiredDays:required,windowEnd,endStage}; } function ignoredStores(km,r){ const chosen = r.store; const chosenKm = chosen ? chosen.km : null; const nextStageEnd = DATA.stageEndsKm[Math.min(89, r.endStage)]; const displayUntil = Math.max(r.windowEnd, chosenKm || 0, nextStageEnd || 0); const rows = ALL_STORES .filter(s => s.km > km && s.km <= displayUntil) .filter(s => { const isChosen=!!chosen&&s.name===chosen.name&&Math.abs(s.km-chosen.km)<0.001; const chosenIsActuallyUsable=r.verdict==="OK"||r.verdict==="SECOURS"||r.verdict==="PLUS PROCHE"||r.verdict==="PLUS T√îT"||r.verdict==="√Ä V√âRIFIER"; return !isChosen||!chosenIsActuallyUsable; }) .sort((a,b)=>a.km-b.km) .map(s=>{ let reason="", cls=""; if(s.role==="EXCLU"){ reason = s.status || "√âcart√©"; cls = "bad"; } else if(s.role==="A_QUALIFIER"){ reason = "Fiabilit√© insuffisante / √† diff=(chosenKm-s.km).toFixed(1); if(s.role==="SECOURS" && r.role==="NORMAL"){ reason = "Avant la cible ¬∑ " + diff + " km avant le magasin choisi"; }else{ reason = "Avant la cible ¬∑ " + diff + " km avant le magasin choisi"; } } else if(s.role==="SECOURS"){ reason = "Avant la cible"; } else if(s.role==="CANDIDATE"){ reason = "Commerce non v√©rifi√©"; } else { reason = "Moins pertinent pour cette fen√™tre"; } return {store:s,reason,cls}; }); document.getElementById("ignoredSummary").textContent="Magasins ignor√©s ("+rows.length+")"; const list=document.getElementById("ignoredList"); list.innerHTML = rows.length ? rows.map(x=>`
${escapeHtml(x.store.name)}
${escapeHtml(x.reason)} ¬∑ km ${x.store.km.toFixed(1)}

'; // Toujours repli√© apr√®s chaque nouveau calcul. document.getElementById("ignoredDetails").open=false; } function escapeHtml(v){ return String(v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({"&":"&","<":"<",">":">",'"':""","'":"'"}[c])); } const STORE_DETOUR_CACHE_KEY='store-detour-cache-v1'; let storeDetourCache=parseJSON(safeStorageGet(STORE_DETOUR_CACHE_KEY))||{}; async function resolveStoreDetour(store){ const direct=Number(store?.offRouteMeters??store?.detourMeters); if(Number.isFinite(direct))return Math.max(0,direct); if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(storeDetourCache,store.name))return storeDetourCache[store.name]; if(!navigator.onLine)return null; try{ const controller=new AbortController(),timer=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),6500); const url='https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?format=jsonv2&limit=1&countrycodes=fr&q='+encodeURIComponent(store.name+' France'); const response=await fetch(url,{signal:controller.signal,headers:{Accept:'application/json'}}); clearTimeout(timer); if(!response.ok)throw Error('geocode'); const rows=await response.json(),row=rows?.[0]; const lat=Number(row?.lat),lon=Number(row?.lon); let value=null; if(Number.isFinite(lat)&&Number.isFinite(lon)){ const p=projectGps(lat,lon); if(p&&Math.abs(p.km-store.km)<=8)value=Math.round(p.distanceMeters/10)*10; } storeDetourCache[store.name]=value; safeStorageSet(STORE_DETOUR_CACHE_KEY,JSON.stringify(storeDetourCache)); return value; }catch(_){return null} } function storeWalkLabel(delta){return 'Dans '+delta.toFixed(1).replace('.',',')+' km de marche'} async function enrichStoreMeta(store,km,baseText,meta){ if(!store||!meta)return; meta.textContent=baseText+detourText; } function show(km,stage,offRoute=null){ lastShown={km,stage,offRoute}; const r=select(km,stage); const box=document.getElementById("result"); const badge=document.getElementById("badge"); const storeEl=document.getElementById("store"); const mini=document.getElementById("resultMini"); const meta=document.getElementById("storeMeta"); const outsideWindow=r.verdict==="HORS FEN√äTRE"||r.verdict===">4 JOURS"; }else meta.textContent="Fin de la Travers√©e"; } ignoredStores(km,r); } function setAutonomyUI(days,notifyMenu=true){ autonomy=Math.max(1,Math.min(4,Number(days)||2)); storeSearchMode='autonomy'; document.querySelectorAll(".day[data-d]").forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle("active",Number(x.dataset.d)===autonomy)); Utilise le km simul√© ou les coordonn√©es manuelles."; } document.getElementById("gpsBtn").addEventListener("click",()=>{ const status=document.getElementById("status"); if(!navigator.geolocation){ status.textContent="GPS non disponible. Utilise le km simul√© ou les coordonn√©es manuelles."; return; } status.textContent="Recherche de la position‚Ä¶"; navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(pos=>{ const lat=pos.coords.latitude,lon=pos.coords.longitude,accuracy=Number(pos.coords.accuracy)||9999; document.getElementById("lat").value=lat.toFixed(6); document.getElementById("lon").value=lon.toFixed(6); if(accuracy>150){ status.textContent="GPS trop impr√©cis (¬±"+Math.round(accuracy)+" m). Position non utilis√©e : r√©essaie ou saisis ton km."; return; } const quality=accuracy>60?"GPS ¬±"+Math.round(accuracy)+" m ¬∑ ":""; const offset=p.distanceMeters>150?" ¬∑ "+Math.round(p.distanceMeters)+" m de la trace":""; status.textContent=quality+"Position : E"+p.stage+" ¬∑ km "+p.km.toFixed(2)+offset; show(p.km,p.stage,p.distanceMeters); },20); },err=>{status.textContent=gpsErrorMessage(err)},{enableHighAccuracy:true,timeout:12000,maximumAge:15000}); }); function simulateStoreKm(){ const input=document.getElementById("storeKmInput"); const km=Number(String(input?.value||"").replace(",",".")); const status=document.getElementById("status"); if(!Number.isFinite(km)||km<0||km>DATA.routeLengthKm){ status.textContent="Saisis un kilom√®tre entre 0 et "+DATA.routeLengthKm.toFixed(0)+"."; return; } const stage=stageFromKm(km); status.textContent="Position simul√©e : E"+stage+" ¬∑ km "+km.toFixed(1); show(km,stage,0); } document.getElementById("storeKmBtn")?.addEventListener("click",simulateStoreKm); document.getElementById("storeKmInput")?.addEventListener("keydown",event=>{ if(event.key==="Enter"){ event.preventDefault(); simulateStoreKm(); } });
